ci-verdict.yml[OQ8M]: F:Aggregates the PR verdict by polling the Checks API: fails unless every expected check on the head SHA is green, naming absent legs | R:code:.github/workflows/gov.yml, code:.github/workflows/dev-ios.yml, code:.github/workflows/dev-android.yml, code:.github/workflows/dev-harmonyos.yml, code:.github/workflows/pages.yml | A:the always-running ci-verdict check on every pull_request | S:An empty or unreadable check snapshot is a failure, never green; absence is judged only after a 120s settling window and is always named — did-not-run is never read as verified. New gating checks must join EXPECTED (gates ios-e2e android-e2e harmonyos-build pages-demo); anything else is printed, never judged. The 70-min deadline outlasts every leg's own timeout (ios 30, harmony 30, android 40).

primitives.md[DB9L]: F:The frozen capability-gateway primitive contract v1.8.0: 26 typed primitives, event channels, permission flags, mandatory audit, and gateway@1 conformance | R:code:contract/primitives.d.ts, code:contract/data-protocols.md, code:docs/ARCHITECTURE.md | A:negotiation string gateway@1; shapes immutable for major v1 — minor bumps are additive only (§8) | S:Unknown error codes are fatal — receivers never fall back on a code they do not recognize.
